Through three numerical studies, we show that PCE distinguishes diagrams with the same persistent entropy, separates causal directions in dynamical systems without constructing a joint persistent diagram, and can be used as a directional topology loss for knowledge distillation.

</details>


### [210] [FraudBench: Protocol-Sensitive Benchmarking of Adversarial Robustness for Financial Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24551)
*Xitong Zeng,Zhaoge Bi,Yitian Yang,Huaming Chen,Quan Z. Sheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FraudBench，一个协议敏感型基准测试，用于金融欺诈和信用风险检测中的对抗性鲁棒性评估，揭示了评估协议的选择会显著影响鲁棒性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融表格数据中的对抗性鲁棒性评估面临领域特定约束、类别不平衡和攻击者能力不对称等挑战，现有方法通常在攻击后做可行性过滤，导致鲁棒性结论不可靠且不一致。

Method: 提出了FraudBench基准，在相同的数据集-模型-攻击-防御设置下，对比三种匹配的评估协议：无约束攻击、事后可行性过滤、部署感知的约束集成攻击。覆盖四个公开金融数据集，评估神经网络、树模型和集成模型，使用三种攻击设置。

Result: 鲁棒性结论高度依赖协议选择。在Lending Club数据集白盒设置下，事后过滤平均仅剩3.7个可行翻转样本，而攻击内投影配合攻击者变异掩码可产生2832.3个可行翻转样本。IEEE-CIS结果显示可行性和攻击者能力是独立的维度，黑盒评估中协议选择可改变模型族排名。

Conclusion: 金融欺诈鲁棒性评估应联合报告预测性能下降和攻击可行性，并将领域约束纳入攻击生成过程，而非作为后处理检查步骤。

Abstract: Machine learning models are widely used in financial fraud and credit-risk detection, yet their adversarial robustness remains difficult to evaluate because financial tabular data involve domain-specific constraints, severe class imbalance, and asymmetric attacker capability. We argue that, in this setting, robustness is not only an attribute of the model, but also an attribute of the evaluation protocol. Different ways of enforcing constraints and capability can lead to substantially different robustness conclusions. This paper presents FraudBench, a protocol-sensitive benchmark for adversarial robustness evaluation in financial fraud and credit-risk detection. Rather than treating domain constraints as post-hoc validity checks, FraudBench evaluates the same dataset--model--attack--defence setting under three matched protocols: unconstrained attacks, post-hoc feasibility filtering, and deployment-aware constraint-integrated attacks. FraudBench covers four public financial datasets, and evaluates neural, tree-based, and ensemble models using three attack settings. Our results show that robustness conclusions are highly protocol-sensitive. On Lending Club Loan Data under the white-box setting, post-hoc filtering leaves only 3.7 feasible-flipped examples on average, whereas in-attack projection with attacker mutability masking produces 2,832.3 feasible-flipped examples under the same perturbation budget. The results on IEEE-CIS further show that feasibility and attacker capability are separate axes, while black-box evaluation shows that protocol choice can alter model-family rankings. These findings suggest that fraud robustness evaluation should report predictive degradation and attack feasibility jointly, and should incorporate domain constraints into attack generation rather than treating them as post-processing checks.

</details>


### [211] [SeisMamba: Low-Latency Single-Station Seismic Magnitude Estimation for Spatially Distributed Earthquake Early Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24561)
*Quenton Yeo,Zhaoge Bi,Linghan Huang,Luke Stephen Higgins,Flora Salim,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeisMamba，一个基于Mamba架构的轻量级单站地震震级快速估计模型，在STEAD基准上取得最优MSE、RMSE和R²，推理速度比Transformer基线快约3倍，并在跨区域泛化测试中保持有效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地震早期预警系统依赖密集区域地震台网和区域特定校准，导致稀疏传感基础设施的高风险地区存在空间覆盖障碍。单站学习方法成本较低，但现有模型面临精度-延迟权衡，且在区域分布偏移下性能退化。

Method: SeisMamba采用层级卷积编码、稀疏选择性状态空间建模、多尺度特征融合，并辅以时间预测头，以支持高效的长序列波形分析，实现从单站三分量地震波形的低延迟震级估计。

Result: 在STEAD基准上SeisMamba在MSE、RMSE和R²指标上优于所有基线，在NVIDIA T4 GPU上批处理32条波形仅需0.55 ms，比Transformer基线快约3倍。智利-台湾区域留出实验中，模型在未见地理区域仍保持有用性能。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间波形建模为空间分布、低成本的地震早期预警提供了有前景的精度-延迟骨干架构。

Abstract: Rapid earthquake magnitude estimation is central to earthquake early warning, yet many operational systems depend on dense regional seismic networks and region-specific calibration. This creates a spatial coverage barrier for high-risk areas with sparse sensing infrastructure. Single-station learning offers a lower-cost alternative, but existing models often face an accuracy--latency trade-off and may degrade under regional distribution shift. We present SeisMamba, a lightweight Mamba-based architecture for low-latency magnitude estimation from minimally processed three-component seismic waveforms recorded at a single station. SeisMamba combines hierarchical convolutional encoding, sparse selective state-space modelling, multi-scale feature fusion, and an auxiliary temporal prediction head to support efficient long-sequence waveform analysis. On the STEAD benchmark, SeisMamba achieves the best MSE, RMSE, and $R^2$ among tested baselines while requiring only 0.55 ms for a batch of 32 waveforms on an NVIDIA T4 GPU, making it about three times faster than transformer-based baselines. We further conduct a Chile--Taiwan regional hold-out experiment as a diagnostic test of cross-region deployment, where SeisMamba retains useful performance on geographically unseen seismic regions. These results suggest that selective state-space waveform modelling provides a promising accuracy--latency backbone for spatially distributed, low-cost earthquake early warning.

</details>


### [212] [Across the Loss Landscape with Progressive Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24568)
*Paul Caillon,Christophe Cerisara,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将增量式增长-优化策略视为逐步放松约束的过程，证明了在非退化极小值附近，渐进增长会通过冻结约束产生的体积效应偏向更平坦的盆地，并抑制尖锐盆地。实验验证了这一机制，但也发现曲率降低并不总能转化为更好的测试性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度神经网络在高度非凸、过参数化的损失景观中仍能良好泛化的原因，特别是几何性质与随机优化之间的关系，以及渐进式增长策略如何产生平坦极小值的理论机制。

Method: 将网络增长建模为渐进约束放松：从低维子模型开始，通过解锁嵌套随机子空间逐步扩展可训练参数，同时将正交补空间冻结在初始化值。在标准局部正则性条件下，证明局部子水平集可由椭球近似，并推导出冻结方向的有效曲率，据此解释渐进增长通过体积效应偏向宽盆地、抑制窄盆地。

Result: 在受控玩具景观和ResNet/CIFAR-100真实设置中验证了理论预测：渐进子空间增长确实可靠地产生更平坦的解，但曲率降低并不普遍转化为更好的测试性能，揭示平坦度-泛化连接中的微妙性。

Conclusion: 渐进增长确实会通过冻结约束的体积效应偏向更平坦的最小值，但平坦度与泛化性能之间的关系比以往认为的更加复杂，不能简单地将更平坦等同于更好的泛化。

Abstract: Deep neural networks generalize well despite their highly nonconvex, overparameterized loss landscapes, a phenomenon often associated with the geometry of the minima found by stochastic optimization. We study how incremental grow-and-optimize strategies bias training toward flatter regions by viewing growth as progressive constraint relaxation. Starting from a low-dimensional submodel, we iteratively expand the trainable parameters by unlocking nested random subspaces while freezing the orthogonal complement at the network initialization, re-optimizing after each expansion until the full architecture is reached. Under standard local regularity conditions around non-degenerate minima, we prove that local sublevel sets are well approximated by ellipsoids and that basin accessibility under frozen constraints can be characterized by an explicit effective curvature in the frozen directions. This leads to an explanation of the bias: progressive growth increases the relative weight of wide basins and suppresses sharp ones through a volume effect induced by the frozen constraints. We empirically validate these predictions in controlled toy landscapes and in a realistic ResNet/CIFAR-100 setting and confirm that although progressive subspace growth reliably produces flatter solutions, curvature reductions do not universally translate into improved test performance, highlighting subtleties in the flatness-generalization connection. The code is available at https://github.com/p0lcAi/Across-the-Loss-Landscape.

</details>


### [213] [IAPO: Influence-Aware Policy Optimization for Credit Assignment in Multi-Turn Service Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24588)
*Bo Ren,Yirong Mao,Yi Yang,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为IAPO（Influence-Aware Policy Optimization）的方法，通过将每次轨迹表示为类型化的影响依赖图，将轨迹级别的优势信号重新分配到各个智能体动作上，从而解决多轮交互中服务智能体的信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在多轮交互中处理长时任务时，相关信息随时间逐步展开而非初始提示中完全给出，因此仅靠最终奖励无法判断哪些动作对任务解决有贡献，需要更精细的信用分配机制。

Method: IAPO将每次rollout表示为类型化的影响依赖图（typed influence-dependency graph），图中包含可训练智能体动作节点，以用户和工具观察作为证据。通过支持使用（support-use）和失败使用（failed-use）结构转换为路由权重，将同一轨迹级优势重新分配给各个动作。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上，IAPO在三个服务智能体基准（τ²-Bench、UserBench、AgentChangeBench）上超过多轮强化学习基线，且在BFCL-v4 Multi-Turn上不损害多轮函数调用性能。

Conclusion: IAPO推进了多轮用户交互中信用分配的理解，为从稀疏结果反馈中训练服务智能体提供了有原则的方法。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly solve long-horizon tasks through multi-turn interactions with users and external tools. In these settings, relevant task information often unfolds over time rather than being fully specified at the initial prompt. Service agents make this challenge especially concrete: users may clarify or revise their goals, while tool responses provide information needed for subsequent decisions. Thus, a final reward alone cannot indicate which actions contributed to resolving the task. Recent methods rely on comparative evidence from other trajectories or resampled continuations, or on separately constructed step-level learning signals, to refine credit. However, a completed rollout already records how information and errors flow between agent actions. We introduce Influence-Aware Policy Optimization (IAPO), which represents each rollout as a typed influence-dependency graph over trainable agent actions, with user and tool observations serving as evidence. IAPO converts support-use and failed-use structure into routing weights that redistribute the same trajectory-level advantage. Experiments with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B demonstrate superior performance over multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) baselines across three service-agent benchmarks: {τ^2}-Bench, UserBench, and AgentChangeBench. BFCL-v4 Multi-Turn further shows that these gains do not compromise multi-turn function-calling performance. This work advances the understanding of credit assignment in multi-turn user interactions and provides a principled approach to training service agents from sparse outcome feedback.

</details>


### [214] [Delayed Optimizer-State Transport Shapes Short-Horizon Training Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24593)
*Jinhui Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自适应优化器（如AdamW）中矩状态保留梯度历史所带来的"延迟传输"效应，发现优化器记忆与近期未来数据顺序的交互作用会影响短期决策，提供了基于机制的有限时域干预判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应优化器在矩变量中保留梯度历史，导致损失加权中的局部变化可能影响后续更新。作者希望探究这种延迟传输是否足够大，以至于会改变短期前瞻决策。

Method: 在提交的未来小批次序列上，通过完整模型-优化器状态对八步AdamW轨迹进行微分，选择曝光匹配的Math-Code损失调度，在12个未使用的0.3M Transformer历史上进行独立评估。同时进行了交叉检查点-未来路径测试和Ising-CNN对照实验。

Result: 全传输在10/12个历史中降低了令牌不相交损失（平均收益4.71×10⁻⁴，单侧符号检验p=0.0193）。两个控制器作用频率相同但在60/96窗口中选择不同调度。删除矩状态传输会破坏准确响应预测。全传输分数在更大候选库中集中精确回放赢家。

Conclusion: 在承诺的短期路径上，优化器记忆和近期未来数据顺序是可操作的训练状态组件，提供了判断何时需要有限时域而非一步干预的机制性标准。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers retain gradient history in moment variables, allowing a local change in loss weighting to alter later updates. We examine whether this delayed transport is large enough to change prospective short-horizon decisions. On committed future-minibatch sequences, we differentiate eight-step AdamW trajectories through the complete model--optimizer state and select exposure-matched Math--Code loss schedules before independent evaluation. Across 12 unused 0.3M Transformer histories, full transport lowers token-disjoint loss relative to an optimizer-aware immediate derivative in 10/12 histories (mean benefit $4.71\times10^{-4}$; exact one-sided sign test, $p=0.0193$). The two controllers act equally often but select different schedules in 60/96 windows. Crossed checkpoint--future-path tests attribute this reordering to the interaction between optimizer state and near-future data, while an independent Ising--CNN experiment shows that deleting moment-state transport destroys accurate response prediction. Full-transport scores also concentrate exact-rollout winners in larger candidate libraries, focusing finite-amplitude evaluation on a shortlist. On these committed short paths, optimizer memory and near-future data order are therefore actionable components of the training state, providing a mechanism-based criterion for when finite-horizon rather than one-step intervention is required.

</details>


### [215] [Taming foundation model with invariance-oriented pre-training for broad-spectrum EEG analysis across signal-level, brain-state, and brain-health tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24597)
*Yulong Dou,Han Wu,Guo Chen,Fangmao Ju,Zhiming Cui,Dinggang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了INCEPT，一个基于不变性学习的脑电（EEG）基础模型，在超过11,000小时的未标注临床脑电数据上训练，通过学习表征级稳定性而非单纯信号重建，在30项线性探测指标中26项排名第一，在30项微调指标中24项排名第一，超越了现有的重建导向EEG基础模型和任务专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG模型大多遵循单数据集单模型的有监督范式，而近期EEG基础模型多数以重建为中心，假设可从局部上下文预测的EEG内容必然是可迁移的神经信息，但这一假设未必成立。作者希望探索一种基于不变性学习的新范式，以构建更具可复用性的EEG基础模型。

Method: INCEPT采用不变性导向（invariance-oriented）的预训练策略，在超过11,000小时的未标注临床EEG数据上训练。其核心思想是学习跨相关EEG观测的表征级稳定性，将稳定的神经结构和对受试者敏感的关键信息与头皮记录中占主导的噪声变异性分离开来，同时保留受试者、状态和条件判别性信息。

Result: 在涵盖信号级评估、脑状态解码和脑健康评估三个层面的十个数据集的广谱基准测试中，INCEPT在30项线性探测指标中26项排名第一，在30项微调指标中24项排名第一，并超越了多个任务专用编码器。消融实验和表征分析表明，不变性导向预训练优于单纯重建导向的预训练。

Conclusion: 不变性学习是构建可复用EEG基础模型的有效原则，INCEPT证明了超越信号重建的不变性预训练能够更好地提升迁移性能并组织受试者敏感的神经表征。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a widely used window into human brain function, but most EEG models remain tied to a one-dataset-one-model supervised paradigm. Recent EEG foundation models offer a route toward reusable representations, but most remain reconstruction-centered, assuming that EEG content predictable from local context is necessarily transferable neural information. Here we present INCEPT, an invariance-oriented EEG foundation model trained on over 11,000 hours of unlabelled clinical EEG. Rather than prioritizing signal recovery alone, INCEPT learns representation-level stability across correlated EEG observations, separating stable neural structure and essential subject-sensitive information from the nuisance variability that dominates scalp recordings while preserving subject-, state- and condition-discriminative information. We evaluate INCEPT on a broad-spectrum benchmark of ten datasets spanning three levels of post-acquisition EEG analysis: signal-level assessment, brain-state decoding, and brain-health evaluation. INCEPT ranks first among recent EEG foundation models on 26 of 30 linear-probing metrics and 24 of 30 fine-tuning metrics, and also surpasses strong task-specific specialist encoders across diverse downstream settings. Objective ablations and representation analyses further show that invariance-oriented pre-training improves transfer and organizes subject-sensitive neural representations beyond reconstruction alone. These results establish invariance learning as a promising principle for building reusable EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [216] [Conditional GraphGANFed: Optimizing Graph-Structured Molecule Generation in Federated Generative Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24610)
*Daniel Manu,Abee Alazzwi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cGraphGANFed，在GraphGANFed基础上引入条件生成机制和批评网络，使联邦GAN能根据用户定义指标生成优化目标的新分子，并在多个指标上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphGANFed虽然结合了联邦学习和图卷积网络用于分子生成，但无法生成针对用户特定指标优化的合成分子，限制了其在药物发现中的应用。

Method: 在GraphGANFed中引入批评网络（critic network），用于评估生成分子是否符合用户定义的指标；将批评网络和判别器的评估结果整合到生成器的损失函数中，引导生成器在保持化学性质相似的同时优化用户指定指标。

Result: 在优化七个常用指标的场景下，cGraphGANFed在Validity和LogP上显著优于GraphGANFed，在QED上略有优势；在仅优化QED的场景下，cGraphGANFed生成的分子QED提升超过10%，同时对模式坍塌和非IID数据具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: cGraphGANFed通过条件生成机制有效实现了用户定义指标引导的分子生成，在保持数据隐私的前提下提升了分子生成的质量和定向优化能力。

Abstract: Generative adversarial networks (GANs) have garnered considerable attention in molecular discovery for their ability to generate novel and high-quality molecules. To efficiently train a GAN model while preserving data privacy, GraphGANFed has been proposed to incorporate federated learning and graph convolutional networks into GAN. Yet, GraphGANFed cannot produce synthetic molecules that only optimize a user-defined metric(s) to facilitate the new drug discovery process. To address this issue, we introduce a novel extension to GraphGANFed, namely conditional GraphGANFed (cGraphGANFed), by incorporating the critic network to assess generated molecules using user-defined metric(s). The evaluation results from both the critic network and discriminator are integrated into the loss function of the generator, guiding it to generate novel molecules that maintain similar chemical properties to real ones while optimizing user-defined metrics. Extensive simulations are conducted in two scenarios. First, cGraphGANFed endeavors to optimize all seven commonly used metrics, and the results show that cGraphGANFed significantly outperforms GraphGANFed in Validity and LogP, with a slight advantage in QED, across different settings. Second, cGraphGANFed focuses solely on optimizing QED, and the results show that the synthetic molecules produced by cGraphGANFed can achieve more than 10% improvement in QED than GraphGANFed. Also, the results demonstrate cGraphGANFed has enhanced resilience against mode collapses and performance reduction caused by non-IID data.

</details>


### [217] [Bandit Submodular Maximization under Matroid Constraints: Learning Compressed Exchange Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24627)
*Zongqi Wan,Zhijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在一般拟阵约束下单调子模函数的对抗性bandit最大化问题，提出了一种随机化oracle多项式时间算法，该算法每轮只需一次可行值查询，期望(1-1/e)-regret为Õ(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})，是首个在一般拟阵约束下具有亚线性regret的对抗性bandit子模最大化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗性bandit设置下进行单调子模函数最大化是一个具有挑战性的问题，此前在一般拟阵约束下缺乏亚线性regret的算法。作者旨在填充这一空白。

Method: 将问题视为学习Poisson base walk的交换策略，从而将其与上下文bandit问题联系起来；引入"balanced fractional exchanges"技术，将指数多个策略的混合压缩为单个分数基，在保留Poisson分析所需交换信息的同时实现多项式时间算法。

Result: 得到期望(1-1/e)-regret为Õ(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})的多项式时间随机化算法，每轮仅需一次可行值查询，是首个在一般拟阵约束下实现亚线性regret的对抗性bandit子模最大化算法。

Conclusion: 该工作通过巧妙的策略压缩技术，首次在一般拟阵约束的对抗性bandit子模最大化中实现了亚线性regret，同时保持了计算效率，为该方向后续研究奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: We study adversarial bandit maximization of monotone submodular functions under a matroid constraint. For a rank-$k$ matroid on $n$ elements, we give a randomized oracle-polynomial algorithm that makes one feasible value query per round and has expected $(1-1/e)$-regret $\widetilde O(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})$. This is the first sublinear-regret algorithm for adversarial bandit submodular maximization under general matroid constraints.
  Technically, we view the problem as learning an exchange policy for the Poisson base walk. This connects the problem to contextual bandits and gives an information-theoretic sublinear-regret guarantee, but directly learning the exponentially many policies requires exponential time and space. We therefore introduce \emph{balanced fractional exchanges}, which compress the policy mixture into a single fractional base while retaining the exchange information needed by the Poisson analysis. This leads to an polynomial time algorithm with the same regret guarantee.

</details>


### [218] [Data Leakage Inflates Generalizability of Power Outage Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24665)
*Yamil Essus,Ranga Raju Vatsavai,Benjamin Rachunok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估了电力中断预测模型在不同测试策略（随机划分、留一州、留一事件）下的泛化能力，发现随机划分的性能被空间和时间自相关所夸大，在真实部署条件下面临严重性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的电力中断预测模型评估实践掩盖了模型在气候驱动基础设施风险评估等新场景下的泛化能力，需要识别影响模型泛化能力的方法论选择。

Method: 使用美国东海岸2018-2023年公开数据，结合天气再分析、土地覆盖数据和GeoAI基础模型（Prithvi WxC）嵌入特征，在多种测试选择策略（随机划分、留一州、留一事件）下比较不同方法论决策对预测性能的影响。

Result: 随机训练测试划分表现出强性能，但该结果被空间和时间自相关所夸大；在空间和时间留出实验中，预测精度大幅下降，模型经常无法超越简单零基线。GeoAI基础模型嵌入仅带来有限且不一致的改进，主要限于空间泛化，无法解决事件级迁移能力差的问题。

Conclusion: 鉴于当前数据可用性和评估实践，公开训练的停电预测模型提供的运营价值有限且不确定；进展需要改进数据覆盖、更现实的评估协议，以及关注结构性数据约束而非边际建模改进。

Abstract: Power outage prediction models are increasingly used in assessments of climate-driven infrastructure risk, yet current evaluation practices obscure whether these models generalize to the novel conditions such applications require. We identify three common methodological choices in power outage prediction models that influence their ability to generalize across spatial, temporal, and event-based settings. We compare the predictive performance impacts of different methodological decisions using publicly available data for the U.S. East Coast from 2018 to 2023 and feature sets derived from weather reanalysis and land-cover data, and embeddings from a GeoAI foundation model (Prithvi WxC). Specifically, we assess model performance under multiple test selection strategies, including unfiltered random splits, leave-one-state-out, and leave-one-event-out designs, which increasingly approximate real-world deployment conditions. While random train-test splits yield strong performance, we show that these results are inflated by spatial and temporal autocorrelation. Under spatial and temporal holdout experiments, predictive accuracy degrades substantially, with models often failing to outperform a simple null baseline. Incorporating GeoAI foundation model embeddings yields limited and inconsistent improvements, primarily for spatial generalization, and does not resolve poor event-level transferability. These findings suggest that, given current data availability and evaluation practices, publicly trained outage prediction models offer limited and uncertain operational value. Progress will likely require improved data coverage, more realistic evaluation protocols, and a shift in focus from marginal modeling advances toward addressing structural data constraints.

</details>


### [219] [A Multimodal Foundation Model for Longitudinal Patient Representation and Scalable Insight Generation in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24688)
*Eugene Vorontsov,Yi Kan Wang,Alican Bozkurt,Adam Casson,Ludmila Tydlitatova,Michal Zelechowski,Ezra E. W. Cohen,Jyoti D. Patel,Max Banaszak,Caitlin McWilliams,Shane Colley,Kate Sasser,Ryan Fukushima,Eric Lefkofsky,Razik Yousfi,Siqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了oFM，一个基于167万真实癌症患者队列构建的基础模型，整合临床轨迹与DNA、RNA和H&E病理等多模态数据，通过编码每日临床和分子事件并随时间整合生成患者状态嵌入，在预后和疗效预测基准上显著优于专家精选的临床和分子基线特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学需要一个能够捕捉癌症演变和治疗过程的纵向患者状态模型，整合多模态观察数据，以更好地支持治疗反应预测、生存分析和药物开发。

Method: 在167万癌症患者的真实队列上训练基础模型oFM，患者级别分区用于训练/验证/测试。模型将每日临床和分子事件（DNA、RNA、H&E病理）编码并随时间整合产生患者状态嵌入，并采用证据支撑的时间图进行机制发现解释。

Result: 在预后基准中，oFM在治疗反应、无进展生存期和总生存期的AUC上显著提升（总生存期0.774 vs 0.563）。在11个比较治疗队列中，oFM嵌入实现了三倍更高的合并和标准化治疗获益AUTOC，9/11队列的获益排序改善，且在两种治疗组内均提供更强的预后区分能力。

Conclusion: oFM作为肿瘤学多模态纵向基础模型，在预后和个体化治疗获益预测方面显著优于传统基线特征，其可解释的机制发现框架有助于临床和药物开发应用。

Abstract: Precision oncology necessitates a longitudinal model of patient state that captures cancer evolution and treatment over time, integrating multimodal observations. We introduce the oFM, a foundation model developed on a real-world oncology cohort of 1.67 million cancer patients that integrates clinical trajectories with DNA, RNA, and H&E pathology. Patient-level partitions were reserved for training, validation, and testing, with over one million patients used for training. The oFM encodes daily clinical and molecular episodes and, along with pathology images, integrates them over time to produce a patient state embedding. We evaluate frozen oFM embeddings against expert-curated clinical and molecular baseline features. In prognostic benchmarks, the oFM improved AUC for treatment response, progression-free survival, and overall survival (0.774 vs. 0.563 for overall survival). Across 11 comparative-treatment cohorts, the oFM embeddings achieved a three-fold higher pooled and scale-normalized treatment-benefit AUTOC than baseline features with improved benefit ranking in 9 of 11 cohorts, and provided stronger prognostic discrimination within both treatment arms. We also evaluated a mechanism discovery framework that interprets downstream models built on oFM embeddings by linking their predicted outcomes to clinically and biologically grounded mechanisms through an evidence-grounded temporal graph, enabling evaluation in clinical and drug-development applications.

</details>


### [220] [On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24696)
*Wenze Lin,Jiale Zhao,Xitai Jiang,Songde Rao,Yining Li,Shenzhi Wang,Bingxiang He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OPDVR方法，通过ReLU门控机制将采样token级OPD的隐式奖励与轨迹正确性对齐，无需引入额外超参数即可无缝结合OPD与RLVR，在六个推理基准上优于标准OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR提供稀疏的任务级反馈，而OPD提供密集的token级指导但忽略轨迹正确性且受限于教师模型性能。现有两者结合的方案依赖加权组合或启发式切换，引入额外超参数和权衡，缺乏简洁有效的统一方法。

Method: 1) 基于轨迹正确性重新公式化采样token级OPD的隐式奖励；2) 应用ReLU门控机制，确保正确轨迹获得非负奖励、错误轨迹获得非正奖励；3) 将修改后的采样token级OPD转化为规范的RLVR方法，可与任意策略梯度算法（如GRPO）结合。

Result: 在六个推理基准上的实验表明，OPDVR一致优于标准OPD。

Conclusion: OPDVR通过简洁的ReLU门控机制实现了OPD与RLVR的无缝融合，在保留教师分布指导的同时对齐任务级正确性，且不引入额外超参数，可作为推理任务后训练的有效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have become two widely adopted paradigms for post-training large language models. However, RLVR suffers from sparse task-level feedback, while OPD provides dense token-level guidance but ignores trajectory correctness, limiting its performance to that of the teacher. Combining them is a promising direction: OPD supplies dense supervisory signals, while RLVR provides task-level correctness. Nevertheless, existing integrations often rely on weighted combination or heuristic switching, introducing extra hyperparameters and trade-offs. We propose On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward (OPDVR), a simple yet effective method that seamlessly combines OPD and RLVR without adding any hyperparameters. We first reformulate the implicit reward of sampled-token OPD based on trajectory correctness, then apply a ReLU gating mechanism to ensure that correct trajectories receive non-negative rewards and incorrect ones receive non-positive rewards---thereby aligning the distillation signal with task success while preserving the teacher's distributional guidance. Furthermore, our modification transforms sampled-token OPD into a proper RLVR method, making it readily combinable with any policy gradient algorithm, such as GRPO. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that OPDVR consistently outperforms standard OPD. Our code is available at https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/OPDVR.

</details>


### [221] [Single State Update Predictive Coding training for Time Series Forecasting and Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24697)
*Matteo Cardoni,Sam Leroux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将生成式预测编码网络（PCN）与支持编码PCN配对训练的方法，通过并行匹配神经激活来避免传统PCN中顺序反向误差传播的瓶颈，并将其应用于时间序列异常检测，实现了更稳定、连续、在线的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码（PC）是一种支持神经网络层并行更新的学习范式，但PCN的主要瓶颈在于顺序的反向误差传播，这限制了其训练效率和在线学习能力。

Method: 提出一种训练技术，将生成式PCN与支持编码PCN配对，两个PCN并行训练以匹配彼此的神经激活，从而避免顺序传播。将该方法应用于时间序列异常检测。

Result: 该方法在时间序列异常检测中表现出更稳定、连续、在线的学习效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的并行PCN配对训练方案有效解决了PCN顺序传播的瓶颈，为时间序列异常检测提供了更稳定的在线学习方案。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) is a neural learning paradigm that enables parallelizable neural network layer updates. However, the main bottleneck of PC Networks (PCN) is the sequential backwards error propagation. To tackle this, we introduce a training technique that pairs a Generative PCN with a support Encoding PCN. The two PCNs are trained in parallel to match their neural activations, without sequential propagation. We apply this to time series anomaly detection and show that our approach results in more stable, continuous, online learning.

</details>


### [222] [Parameter-Level Attribution of Symmetry in Trained Networks Though Parameter-Wise Functional Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24700)
*Alan Muriithi,Vedanta Thapar,Torben Berndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究当一个网络学习了具有已知对称性的函数后，这种对称性是否可以通过参数空间中的运动来实现（即参数空间是否存在实现函数空间群作用的运动），并将其形式化为实现在映射Φ的提升问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解已训练网络中对称性的参数表示机制，探索对称性能否在参数空间中实现，这对于可解释性和对称性泛化具有重要意义。

Method: 将问题形式化为提升问题，提出平滑参数空间作用存在的充要条件（函数对称轨道切线空间须落在参数功能敏感度张成的空间中），并在最小二乘松弛下推导出两个局部参数方向：一个跟随对称轨道，一个朝向等变子空间下降。

Result: 在旋转不变分类器和哈密顿神经网络上验证：重算的方向能跟踪轨道并减少等变缺陷，而固定方向在训练后偏离两者。

Conclusion: 对称性运动可以通过参数空间局部实现，但仅具有局部有效性；架构未显式强制对称性时该结论依然成立。

Abstract: When a network has learned a function with a known symmetry, can that symmetry be moved through the parametrisation---is there a motion in parameter space realising the group action in function space? We formulate this as a lifting problem for the realisation map $Φ:θ\mapsto f_θ$, and show that a smooth parameter-space action exists only if the tangent space to the function's symmetry orbit lies within the image of $\mathrm dΦ_θ$, whose columns are the \emph{functional sensitivities} of individual parameters. This condition is also sufficient for pointwise first-order lifting. Relaxing it in least squares yields two local parameter directions: one following the symmetry orbit, one descending towards the equivariant subspace, with residuals measuring what the parametrisation cannot reach. On a rotationally invariant classifier we find these directions induce their predicted function-space motion, but only locally: recomputed directions track the orbit and reduce the equivariance defect, while directions held fixed depart from both after training. The same holds for Hamiltonian neural networks trained on a rotationally symmetric potential, even though the architecture does not explicitly enforce the symmetry.

</details>


### [223] [Constrained Hyperparameter Optimization for Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24712)
*Bruno Veloso,João Gama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究数据流场景下在线优化算法中边界约束处理策略问题，提出了四种优于传统"边界"策略的方法，并在公开数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数优化对模型性能至关重要，但现有研究多集中于批量学习场景。数据流场景下需要在线自适应调整超参数，而许多超参数受限于有界搜索空间，需要有效的边界约束处理技术来纠正无效解。

Method: 提出了四种处理在线优化算法中边界约束的策略，不同于传统方法中将超出阈值的数值直接对齐到边界值的"边界"策略。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实证研究表明，所提出的四种边界策略在性能上优于传统的"边界"策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的边界约束处理策略能有效提升在线优化算法在数据流场景下的超参数自调整能力，优于传统边界策略。

Abstract: Optimization of hyperparameters is a critical factor to obtain optimal model performance. While existing research has predominantly concentrated on batch-learning scenarios, addressing the complexities inherent in data streams presents a challenge. The deployment of sophisticated methodologies to manage data streams becomes highly important. Consequently, the capacity for self-adjusting hyperparameters during on-line learning phases emerges as a goal. Many hyperparameters exhibit constraints and are confined within bounded search spaces, rendering specific solutions unacceptable upon applying optimization operators. To solve this issue, employing boundary constraint- handling techniques becomes imperative to rectify invalid solutions. This paper presents strategies for effectively managing boundary constraints within constrained numerical optimization problems. Recent methodologies, including heuristic and evolutionary-based optimization, employ a "boundary" strategy, wherein values that surpass boundary thresholds for a given hyperparameter are realigned to the respective limits. Our study introduces four strategies to navigate boundary constraints in online optimization algorithms. Through empirical investigations conducted on established datasets, we demonstrate that adopting boundary strategies outperforms the "boundary" strategy.

</details>


### [224] [Enhancing Bayesian Optimization and Active Learning Through Kernel Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24721)
*Heng Zhang,Haotian Xiang,Qin Lu,Konstantinos D. Polyzos,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KENDO框架，用核集成与自适应贝叶斯加权替代超参数采样，统一贝叶斯优化与贝叶斯主动学习，在提升性能的同时大幅降低计算开销（BO最高5倍、AL最高27倍加速）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化和贝叶斯主动学习中的超参数选择是关键挑战，模型误设会导致次优性能，而完全贝叶斯处理方法通常依赖计算昂贵的MCMC采样，需要一种更高效且稳健的统一方法。

Method: 提出KENDO框架，核心思路是将超参数采样替换为核集成（EGP）与自适应贝叶斯加权，结合分歧感知（disagreement-aware）采集策略，统一实现KENDO-BO（用于贝叶斯优化）和KENDO-AL（用于贝叶斯主动学习），并通过随机标量化扩展到多目标优化。

Result: 在合成及真实基准上测试表明：(i) KENDO-BO相比最先进方法达到竞争性或更优的优化性能，计算开销降低最高5倍；(ii) KENDO-AL相比基于MCMC的主动学习基准实现更优的预测校准，速度提升最高27倍。

Conclusion: KENDO框架验证了核集成结合分歧感知采集策略能够在保持甚至提升性能的同时显著降低计算成本，为贝叶斯优化和主动学习提供了一种高效、统一且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Hyperparameter selection remains a key challenge in Bayesian optimization (BO) and Bayesian active learning (AL), as model misspecification can lead to suboptimal performance, while more accurate fully Bayesian treatments typically rely on computationally expensive MCMC sampling. This paper proposes a unified framework, KENDO (Kernel ENsemble Disagreement-aware Operator), that integrates Ensemble Gaussian Processes (EGP) with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. The central idea is to replace hyperparameter sampling with a kernel ensemble and adaptive Bayesian weighting, combined with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. Within this unified framework, we instantiate KENDO-BO for BO and KENDO-AL for Bayesian AL, demonstrating that both arise from a common self-correcting mechanism with task-specific acquisition objectives. We further extend the approach to multi-objective optimization via random scalarization that preserves the single-optimizer conditioning structure. Thorough numerical tests on synthetic and real-world benchmarks across single-objective optimization, multi-objective optimization, and active learning demonstrate that (i) KENDO-BO achieves competitive or superior optimization performance compared to state-of-the-art methods while reducing computational overhead by up to $5\times$ and (ii) KENDO-AL achieves superior predictive calibration over MCMC-based active learning baselines with up to $27\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [225] [Parameter-Efficient Self-Supervised Adaptation for EEG-FM under Fixed Computational Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24727)
*Meghal Dani,Stefanie Liebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究参数高效的自我监督适应方法在EEG基础模型中的应用，仅更新9%的参数即可实现跨临床数据集的良好泛化，在三个临床EEG数据集上相比线性探测最高提升20倍AUCPR，且固定计算预算下只需20-50%的未标注数据即可达到峰值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型通过自监督预训练虽然承诺可迁移的表征，但在多样化临床数据集上的泛化能力有限，而全量微调在资源受限的临床场景中因计算需求过高而不可行。

Method: 采用参数高效的自我监督适应方法，仅更新9%的参数来对齐表征到目标任务；在BIOT（对比学习）和CBraMod（掩码重建）两个不同预训练目标的状态最优模型上进行评估，覆盖TUAB异常检测、TUEV事件分类和CHB-MIT癫痫检测三个临床数据集，并在分布内和分布外条件下测试。

Result: SSL适应相比线性探测带来一致的性能提升，最高达20倍AUCPR；固定计算预算下，仅需20-50%的可用未标注数据即可达到峰值性能；固定总窗口数时，性能对患者数量不敏感，表明性能仅依赖于整体时间窗口多样性。

Conclusion: 参数高效的适应方法使得EEG基础模型在最小计算开销和数据收集负担下即可有效部署，为资源受限的临床场景提供了实用方案。

Abstract: EEG foundation models pretrained via self-supervised learning promise transferable representations, but their generalization remains limited, especially across diverse clinical datasets. Full fine-tuning is impractical for resource-constrained clinical settings due to high computational requirements. In this work, we investigate whether parameter-efficient self-supervised adaptation, updating only 9% of parameters suffices to align representations to target tasks. We evaluate our method on two state-of-the-art models with different pretraining objectives: BIOT (contrastive) and CBraMod (masked reconstruction), and evaluate on three clinical EEG datasets for abnormality detection (TUAB), event classification (TUEV), and seizure detection (CHB-MIT) under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution conditions. SSL adaptation yields consistent gains over linear probing, up to 20x AUCPR. Under a fixed compute budget, peak performance requires only 20--50% of available unlabeled data. Critically, when total window count is fixed, performance remains invariant to patient count, suggesting that performance is dependent on overall temporal window diversity only. Our findings demonstrate that parameter-efficient adaptation enables effective deployment of EEG Foundation models (EEG-FM) with minimal computational overhead and data collection burden. Code available at: https://github.com/c3n-group/efficient-eeg-adapt

</details>


### [226] [Optimal Alternating Regret for Online Learning and Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24731)
*Yixin Tao,Weiqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了在线线性优化（OLO）和在线凸优化（OCO）中最小最大最优的交替遗憾问题，在概率单纯形上实现了O(log d)的常数交替遗憾（不随T增长），在一般OCO上实现了O(d log(1+T/d))的交替遗憾，均匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替遗憾是博弈交替学习动态中自然产生的遗憾概念，此前的结果（如O(log^{2/3}d · T^{1/3})）远未达到最优，本文旨在完全解决这一问题的上下界。

Method: 针对OLO提出新算法实现常数交替遗憾并给出匹配下界；针对一般OCO给出O(d log(1+T/d))的算法并证明Ω(d log(1+T/d))的下界。

Result: OLO场景下交替遗憾为O(log d)常数界（不依赖T），显著优于此前O(log^{2/3}d · T^{1/3})的结果；OCO场景达到O(d log(1+T/d))，优于此前Õ(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})，且证明了下界匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作完全解决了交替遗憾在OLO和OCO中的最小最大最优问题，并由此首次实现了两人一般和博弈中以O(1/T)收敛到粗相关均衡（CCE）的非耦合学习动力学，所有先前工作都带有额外的log T因子。

Abstract: We settle the minimax-optimal alternating regret, a regret notion motivated by alternating learning dynamics in games, for both online linear optimization (OLO) and online convex optimization (OCO).
  For OLO over the probability simplex $Δ_d$, we give an algorithm with $O(\log d)$ alternating regret that remains a constant for any time horizon $T$, and a matching lower bound. Our constant regret bound significantly improves previous results with $O(\log ^{2/3}d \cdot T^{1/3})$ regret [Cevher, Cutkosky, Kavis, Piliouras, Skoulakis, Viano, NeurIPS 2023, Hait, Li, Luo, Zhang, COLT 2025]. As a result, we obtain alternating learning dynamics with $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games and $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to coarse correlated equilibria in two-player general-sum games. This is the first uncoupled learning dynamics with $O(1/T)$ convergence to CCE in two-player general-sum games, while all prior works suffer additional $\log T$ factors.
  For general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set, we give an algorithm with $O(d\log (1+T/d))$ alternating regret, improving the previous best of $\widetilde{O}(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})$. We also prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(d\log (1+T/d))$, showing that the $Ω(\log T)$ factor is unavoidable.

</details>


### [227] [$(\text{DNN})^2$: Doubly Non-Negative Relaxations for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24743)
*Hanna Jiamei Zhang,Alan Papalia,Michael Everett,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为(DNN)²的认证方法，通过特征值最大化过程在非唯一的对偶乘子空间中搜索有效证书，使得双重非负规划（DNN）松弛可以用于ReLU神经网络验证，在保持全局最优性保证的同时提供比标准SDP方法更紧的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LP和SDP松弛方法在ReLU神经网络验证中因松弛间隙过大而给出过于保守的安全保证。虽然完全正规划（CPP）能消除间隙但NP难解，其最便宜的可解松弛——双重非负规划（DNN）虽保留了关键约束，但由于规模过大超出内点法的处理能力。尽管Burer-Monteiro（BM）分解已被用于SDP验证，但尚不存在适用于更紧的DNN公式的结果。

Method: 提出一个新颖的特征值最大化过程，专门搜索非唯一的对偶乘子空间以寻找有效证书（即全局最优性保证），从而使得DNN公式下的BM因子分解能够用于ReLU神经网络验证。

Result: 实验表明(DNN)²方法生成的界始终比标准SDP方法更紧，常常匹配精确解；当有效证书存在时，认证过程能确认全局最优性。

Conclusion: 这项工作为在安全关键的自动驾驶系统中部署神经网络控制器和感知模块，提供了紧密、可认证且计算上可扩展的验证保证的关键步骤。

Abstract: Existing linear program (LP) and semidefinite program (SDP) relaxations for rectified linear unit (ReLU) neural network (NN) verification yield overly-conservative safety guarantees due to significant relaxation gaps. While the completely positive program (CPP) formulation closes this gap, it is NP-hard to solve. Its cheapest tractable relaxation, the doubly non-negative program (DNN), retains critical constraints as an SDP, but one whose size exceeds the reach of interior-point methods at practical scale. While Burer-Monteiro (BM) factorization has been applied to make SDP-based verification scalable, no such result exists for the strictly tighter DNN formulation. A key obstacle is that additional non-negativity constraints in the DNN cause dual multipliers for optimality certification to be non-unique, making standard certification methods inapplicable. We propose a novel eigenvalue maximization procedure that searches the non-unique multiplier space for a valid certificate, i.e. a global optimality guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that our approach $(\text{DNN})^2$ produces bounds consistently tighter than the standard SDP method, often matching the exact solution, and that our certification procedure confirms global optimality when a valid certificate exists. These results are a key step toward providing tight, certifiable, and computationally scalable verification guarantees needed to deploy neural network controllers and perception modules in safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>


### [228] [Beyond Uniform Local Isometry and Topology: FactoMap for Disentangled Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24762)
*Sohini Gupta,Bahareh Tolooshams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出函数空间结构（Factor-Space Structure）以区分拓扑等价但几何不同的因子空间，并提出Factor-Space Topographic Map（FactoMap）方法来学习可解释原型，从而在表示中保留因子空间的周期性、折叠和非均匀性，实现更好的解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解耦方法通常用欧几里得乘积坐标来表示生成因子，但忽略了底层因子空间可能存在的包裹（wrap）、塌缩（collapse）和随位置变化的几何特性。

Method: 提出因子空间结构（结合因子域、生成器诱导的等价关系和位置依赖的尺度）来区分拓扑等价但几何不同的空间；提出FactoMap，通过地形学习（topographic learning）将因子空间格子的周期性、塌缩和非均匀范围迁移到表示中。

Result: 实验表明，匹配这种结构能够保持因子的连续性，并实现对底层因子的有效解耦。

Conclusion: 统计独立的因子不一定在几何上是可分离的（如色相和尺度产生的效应以不同速率增长），而FactoMap通过保留因子空间结构成功实现了更好的解耦。

Abstract: Many disentanglement methods represent generative factors using Euclidean product coordinates, although the underlying factor spaces may wrap, collapse, or have position-dependent geometry. We introduce factor-space structure, combining factor domains, generator-induced identifications, and position-dependent scales to distinguish topologically equivalent spaces with different factor geometries. We show that statistically independent factors need not be geometrically separable: hue and scale produce effects that grow at different rates, yielding anisotropy that no fixed rescaling removes. We propose the Factor-Space Topographic Map (FactoMap), which learns interpretable prototypes indexed by a factor-space lattice. Topographic learning transfers the lattice's periodicity, collapses, and non-uniform extent to the representation. Experiments show that matching this structure preserves factor continuity and enables disentanglement of the underlying factors.

</details>


### [229] [LION: A Clifford Neural Paradigm for Multimodal-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24795)
*Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Zhengyu Wu,Henan Sun,Daohan Su,Guang Zeng,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LION（Clifford神经范式），基于Clifford代数和分离图神经网络范式（传播-然后-聚合），在多模态属性图上实现对齐-然后-融合的框架，解决现有方法在模态对齐中忽略图上下文和模态融合中缺乏适应性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态图学习（multimodal-attributed graphs）存在两个关键局限：(1) 现有模态对齐器采用拓扑约束或模态特定算子作为tokenizer，忽略图上下文并抑制模态交互；(2) 现有模态融合方法多是对双模态图的简单适配，未能充分利用带拓扑先验的对齐token，导致泛化性差。

Method: LION框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 基于Clifford代数构建模态感知几何流形，通过几何诱导的高阶图传播实现模态交互和模态对齐；(2) 基于对齐token的拓扑感知Clifford分量，提出自适应全息聚合（adaptive holographic aggregation）模块，整合分量能量和传播尺度信息及可学习参数来改进模态融合。

Result: 在9个文本-图像多模态属性图数据集上的大量实验表明，LION在3个图下游任务和3个模态下游任务上显著优于SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: LION通过Clifford代数驱动的几何流形和自适应全息聚合，有效解决了多模态图中模态对齐忽略上下文和模态融合缺乏适应性的问题，验证了其在不同下游任务上的优越性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recently, the rapid advancement of multimodal domains has driven a data-centric paradigm shift in graph ML, transitioning from text-attributed to multimodal-attributed graphs. This advancement significantly enhances data representation and expands the scope of graph downstream tasks, such as modality-oriented tasks, thereby improving the practical utility of graph ML. Despite its promise, limitations exist in the current neural paradigms:(1) Neglect Context in Modality Alignment: Most existing methods adopt topology-constrained or modality-specific operators as tokenizers.These aligners inevitably neglect graph context and inhibit modality interaction, resulting in suboptimal alignment.(2) Lack of Adaptation in Modality Fusion: Most existing methods are simple adaptations for 2-modality graphs and fail to adequately exploit aligned tokens equipped with topology priors during fusion, leading to poor generalizability and performance degradation.To address the above issues, we propose LION (c\underline{LI}ff\underline{O}rd \underline{N}eural paradigm) based on the Clifford algebra and decoupled graph neural paradigm (i.e., propagation-then-aggregation) to implement alignment-then-fusion in multimodal-attributed graphs. Specifically, we first construct a modality-aware geometric manifold grounded in Clifford algebra.This geometric-induced high-order graph propagation efficiently achieves modality interaction, facilitating modality alignment.Then, based on the topology-aware Clifford components of aligned tokens, we propose adaptive holographic aggregation. This module integrates component-wise energy and propagation-scale information with learnable parameters to improve modality fusion. Extensive experiments on 9 text-image MAG datasets demonstrate that LION significantly outperforms SOTA baselines across 3 graph and 3 modality downstream tasks.

</details>


### [230] [MDTE: Minority-Aware Diffusion over Temporal Edge Events for Imbalanced Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24812)
*Zhou Zelong,Zhang Tianming,Yang Zhengyi,Tang Yifu,Hou Chenyu,Cao Bin,Fan Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MDTE，一个面向时序图上类别不平衡节点分类的少数类感知扩散框架，通过条件扩散去噪重建稳定的时序边事件表示，并在五个真实数据集上显著提升少数类指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图上的类别不平衡节点分类面临两个挑战：多数类主导的时序传播会逐步同化少数类表示，而传统的节点和邻居信息对少数类缺乏足够的判别证据。

Method: MDTE包含两个核心模块：(1) 分布感知选择性传播（Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation），结合基于局部离群因子（LOF）的传播过滤与簇感知低频传播，保留有用邻居依赖同时缓解有害传播和多数类信息同化；(2) 多视图判别融合（Multi-View Discriminative Fusion），利用特征重建和拓扑预测刻画类别间分布差异，提取互补判别信号以引导去噪过程。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上，MDTE在少数类指标上持续取得最佳性能：少数类召回率最高提升23.53个百分点，少数类F1提升8.68个百分点，AUPRC提升2.67个百分点。

Conclusion: MDTE通过少数类感知的扩散框架有效解决了时序图上类别不平衡节点分类问题，验证了条件扩散去噪与分布感知传播相结合的可行性。

Abstract: Class-imbalanced node classification on temporal graphs is challenging because majority-dominated temporal propagation progressively assimilates minority representations, while conventional node and neighborhood information provides insufficient discriminative evidence for minority classes. To address these issues, we propose MDTE, a minority-aware diffusion framework that reconstructs stable and discriminative temporal edge-event representations through conditional diffusion denoising. Specifically, MDTE introduces Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation, which combines Local Outlier Factor (LOF)-based propagation filtering with cluster-aware low-frequency propagation. The module preserves informative neighborhood dependencies while mitigating harmful propagation and majority-class information assimilation. It further develops Multi-View Discriminative Fusion, which exploits feature reconstruction and topology prediction to characterize class-wise differences in distribution learning and extracts complementary discriminability signals to guide denoising. Experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that MDTE consistently achieves the best performance on minority-class-oriented metrics, improving minority-class recall by up to 23.53 percentage points, minority-class F1 by 8.68 percentage points, and AUPRC by 2.67 percentage points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [231] [Effective Learning Rate Governs Loss Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24814)
*Zihan Liu,Ruiheng Zheng,Shaobo Zhang,Changxin Tian,Kunlong Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Lei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型预训练中存在ELR坍缩现象：损失动态主要由有效学习率（ELR = LR/参数范数）这一比值决定，而非单独的LR或参数范数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型预训练中，学习率和参数范数如何共同影响损失动态尚不清楚，作者希望找到统一解释LR调度和范数控制作用的共同坐标。

Method: 通过在不同LR和参数范数组合下进行大规模预训练实验，计算ELR（LR除以参数范数），匹配ELR后比较损失轨迹；系统消融研究归一化设计和LR-范数变化时间尺度；通过控制干预（权重衰减和Hyperball）验证ELR的作用机制；将ELR引入功能缩放定律（FSL）进行跨范数控制方法迁移。

Result: 当ELR匹配时，不同LR和参数范数的训练损失轨迹高度重合（误差仅约10^-3量级），且该现象跨优化器、架构、数据集和模型规模一致成立；权重衰减和Hyperball主要通过其诱导的ELR调度影响损失动态；基于ELR的FSL可跨范数控制方法迁移，并能解释延迟加速现象。

Conclusion: ELR是连接LR调度、范数控制和损失动态的统一坐标，为理解和预测语言模型预训练行为提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: We uncover ELR collapse in language model pretraining: learning rate (LR) and parameter norm govern loss dynamics primarily through their ratio, the effective learning rate (ELR). When ELR is matched across runs, their loss trajectories collapse throughout training despite substantially different LRs and parameter norms. Across optimizers, architectures, datasets, and model scales, mean collapse errors are typically a few x 10^-3, below the seed-to-seed variation measured in a representative configuration. Systematic ablations identify normalization design and the timescale of LR-norm variation as key determinants of collapse precision. Controlled interventions further show that weight decay and Hyperball shape loss dynamics primarily through the ELR schedules they induce. Replacing LR with ELR enables a fitted functional scaling law (FSL) to transfer across norm-control methods. The resulting ELR-based FSL also explains delayed acceleration, a recurring effect of norm control. Together, these results establish ELR as a common coordinate linking LR scheduling, norm control, and loss dynamics.

</details>


### [232] [A Geometric Theory of Robust Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24818)
*Binita Maity*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于邻域的公平性审计在扰动下的鲁棒性，提出了几何分析框架，建立了邻域不变性的充分条件，量化了邻域替换如何导致审计不稳定，并引入"审计波动性"指标来衡量公平性审计对扰动的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于邻域的公平性审计被广泛使用，但审计过程本身的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。由于这些审计依赖最近邻关系，特征空间的微小扰动可能改变局部邻域，从而产生不同的公平性评估结果，即使模型预测未发生变化。

Method: 构建了几何框架来分析有界扰动下邻域公平性审计的鲁棒性：建立邻域不变性的充分条件；量化邻域替换向审计不稳定的传播机制；提出审计波动性（audit volatility）作为重复扰动下公平性审计期望敏感度的度量。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验支持理论分析，表明所提出的框架能够解释基于邻域的公平性审计所观察到的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作为邻域公平性审计的鲁棒性提供了理论基础，揭示了审计结果对特征空间扰动的敏感机制，并给出了可量化的稳定性指标。

Abstract: Neighborhood-based fairness audits evaluate individual fairness by comparing predictions among similar individuals in feature space. Despite their widespread use, little is known about the robustness of the auditing procedure itself. Because these audits rely on nearest neighbor relationships, small perturbations in feature space can alter local neighborhoods and produce different fairness assessments even when model predictions remain unchanged. We develop a geometric framework for analyzing the robustness of neighborhood-based fairness audits under bounded perturbations. Our analysis establishes sufficient conditions for neighborhood invariance, quantifies how neighborhood replacement propagates to audit instability, and introduces audit volatility, a measure of the expected sensitivity of fairness audits under repeated perturbations. Experiments on benchmark datasets support the theoretical analysis and show that the proposed framework explains the observed stability of neighborhood-based fairness audits.

</details>


### [233] [BioKERN: Biological Kernel Regularization for Histology-to-Transcriptomics Neighborhood Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24823)
*Seungik Cho,Betul Orcan-Ekmekci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioKERN是一个多模态空间表征学习框架，通过将转录组相似性和空间邻近性结合为可学习的生物学核（biological kernel），为组织学-转录组学多模态学习提供显式的邻域结构监督和嵌入几何正则化，在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组织学-转录组学多模态学习方法过于强调实例级匹配，忽略了非配对点之间共享的分子或空间上下文。空间解析生物学需要保留生物学邻域结构的表征，而非仅追求精确的跨模态对应。

Method: BioKERN构建训练时的生物学核，结合转录组相似性和空间邻近性，提供分级邻域监督并正则化嵌入几何。评估采用固定的、与模型无关的生物学邻域定义，所有方法共享相同的评估标准。

Result: 在Mouse Brain Visium和Human Liver GSE240429数据集上，BioKERN在单尺度和多尺度设置下均持续优于BLEEP。受控共享架构实验表明，大部分改进来自生物学核正则化而非模型容量增加。

Conclusion: 显式的生物学几何可以作为多模态空间生物学学习中可解释的归纳偏置，显著提升生物学邻域检索性能。

Abstract: Spatially resolved biology requires representations that preserve biological neighborhood structure rather than only exact cross-modal correspondences. Existing histology--transcriptomics objectives can emphasize instance-level matching even when non-paired spots share molecular or spatial context. We introduce BioKERN, a multimodal spatial representation-learning framework that incorporates biological structure as an explicit, learnable inductive bias. BioKERN constructs a training-time biological kernel by combining transcriptomic similarity and spatial proximity, then uses it to provide graded neighborhood supervision and regularize embedding geometry. Evaluation uses a fixed, model-independent biological neighborhood definition shared by all methods. Across Mouse Brain Visium and Human Liver GSE240429, BioKERN consistently improves biological-neighborhood retrieval over BLEEP in both single- and multi-scale settings. Controlled shared-architecture experiments show that most of the improvement arises from biological-kernel regularization rather than increased model capacity. These results support explicit biological geometry as an interpretable inductive bias for multimodal learning in spatial biology.

</details>


### [234] [Bellman Calibration for Marginalized Importance Weighting in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24858)
*Lars van der Laan,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等渗贝尔曼校准（Isotonic Bellman Calibration），一种一维、模型无关的后处理方法，用于减少离线策略评估中边际化重要性加权估计的占用平衡违规（occupancy-balance violations），同时保留初始占用比率估计的排序信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最小极大、原始对偶和拟合不动点估计器由于函数类近似、正则化或不完全优化，会留下残差占用平衡违规。这些违规难以诊断和减少，因为目标函数缺乏直接的监督验证损失用于超参数调优、模型选择和早停。

Method: 该方法通过在一维非递减变换类上应用拟合占用比率评估（FORE）来校正估计的尺度和形状。贝尔曼校准被刻画为一种条件不动点性质，等价于针对校准比率的所有测试函数满足占用平衡。文中还推导了校准-改进界（calibration-refinement bound）。

Result: 建立了等渗贝尔曼校准的有限样本校准保证和相对于初始估计最佳单调变换的KL神谕不等式。该方法在校准误差和KL风险上达到统计误差内接近最佳单调校正的性能，并为下游目标占用泛函（包括策略价值估计）提供保证。

Conclusion: 等渗贝尔曼校准实现了小校准误差和KL风险（在最佳单调校正的统计误差范围内），并提供了对下游目标占用泛函（包括策略价值估计）的保证，是一种有效且实用的离线策略评估后处理工具。

Abstract: Marginalized importance weighting evaluates a target policy by reweighting offline state-action samples with its discounted occupancy ratio, characterized by an adjoint Bellman equation. Existing minimax, primal-dual, and fitted fixed-point estimators can leave residual occupancy-balance violations because of function-class approximation, regularization, or incomplete optimization. These violations are difficult to diagnose and reduce because the objectives generally lack a direct supervised validation loss for hyperparameter tuning, model selection, and early stopping. We introduce isotonic Bellman calibration, a one-dimensional, model-agnostic post-processing method that reduces these violations while preserving the ranking information in any initial occupancy-ratio estimate. The method corrects the estimate's scale and shape by applying fitted occupancy-ratio evaluation (FORE) over a one-dimensional class of nondecreasing transformations. We characterize Bellman calibration as a conditional fixed-point property equivalent to occupancy-balance against every test function of the calibrated ratio. More generally, we derive a calibration-refinement bound showing that any fitted ratio with small calibration error performs nearly as well as the best post-processing based on its fitted values. For isotonic Bellman calibration, we establish finite-sample calibration guarantees and a KL oracle inequality relative to the best monotone transformation of the initial estimate. Consequently, isotonic Bellman calibration achieves small calibration error and KL risk within statistical error of the best monotone correction, with guarantees for downstream target-occupancy functionals, including policy-value estimation.

</details>


### [235] [Improving Cross-Problem Vehicle Routing with Locally Augmented Preferences and Representation Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24859)
*Arthur Corrêa,Paulo Nascimento,Samuel Moniz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出POLAR训练算法与PLE编码器架构，分别通过局部搜索增强偏好对和渐进分层专家路由，解决多任务车辆路径问题（VRP）求解器在训练监督信号不足和编码器纠缠表示两方面的局限，在16个分布内变体上平均gap降低21.3%，并在27/32个未见变体上超越先前神经方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经多任务VRP求解器存在两个关键瓶颈：1）训练方面，强化学习面临奖励尺度差异和策略改进后优势信号萎缩的问题，而偏好优化受限于策略自身生成解的质量，两者都缺乏强监督；2）架构方面，全共享编码器将异构变体的约束相关表示纠缠在一起，限制了跨问题泛化能力。

Method: 提出两个模型无关的贡献：1）POLAR（Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement）训练算法，在构造偏好对之前对最优解码路径施加局部搜索精化，从而产生信息量更丰富的成对边际；2）PLE（Progressive Layered Extraction）编码器，通过门控机制将每个编码层路由到共享专家和任务特定专家，渐进分离通用路由结构与约束特定编码。

Result: 在16个分布内VRP变体上，相对于最强发表基线将参考解的平均gap降低21.3%；在32个未见变体中的27个上超越先前神经方法。消融实验证实两个贡献均有效，且在不同骨干模型架构上均提升跨问题泛化能力。

Conclusion: POLAR和PLE的组合显著提升了神经多任务VRP求解器的性能与泛化能力，两个贡献相互补充，分别从训练信号和架构表征两个角度解决了多任务VRP求解的关键难题。

Abstract: Multi-task vehicle routing problem (VRP) solvers seek to handle multiple VRP variants within a single unified model, avoiding the need to train a separate model for every variant. In spite of recent progress, current approaches remain limited on two fronts. On the training side, reinforcement learning suffers from reward-scale disparities and shrinking advantage signals as policies improve, whereas preference optimization stagnates once sampled tours become near-identical and thus fundamentally limited by the quality of the policy's own generated solutions, leaving both paradigms with weak supervision as training progresses. On the architecture side, existing fully shared encoders entangle constraint-dependent representations across heterogeneous variants, which limits generalization. We address these gaps with two model-agnostic contributions. First, we propose Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement (POLAR), a novel training algorithm that applies a local search refinement pass to the best decoded tour before forming preference pairs, yielding much more informative pairwise margins. Second, a Progressive Layered Extraction (PLE) encoder routes each encoder layer through one shared expert and a set of task-specific experts via a gating mechanism, progressively separating common routing structure from constraint-specific encodings. Through extensive experiments on various VRP variants, we show that POLAR and PLE together elevate the current state-of-the-art among neural multi-task solvers. We reduce the average gap to reference solutions by 21.3% relative to the strongest published baseline on 16 in-distribution variants, and outperform prior neural methods on 27 out of 32 unseen variants. Ablation studies confirm the efficacy of each contribution, showing that both improve cross-problem generalization across multiple backbone model architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Markets, Not Planners: Decentralized Orchestration of LLM Agents with Private Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23867)
*Xiao Liu,Haoyang Li,Songwei Li,Hongbo Fang,Fengli Xu,Feng Shi,James Evans*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: AgentLance是一个基于重复劳动力市场的多智能体编排框架，通过让代理基于私有成本和策略笔记竞标任务，采用VCG风格支付规则实现去中心化的任务分配，在多项任务上优于单模型和集中式编排基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体编排采用集中式规划器分配任务，存在瓶颈、需要私有信息且易被操纵，无法适应日益增长的智能体池和多样化能力需求。

Method: 提出AgentLance：设计一个重复劳动力市场机制，智能体基于私有成本和自我维护的策略笔记竞标任务，分配器根据竞标和公开声誉记录选择赢家，采用VCG风格支付规则奖励成本感知的竞标；复杂任务通过层级委托分解并分包。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成、知识密集型QA和智能体任务上，AgentLance将智能体匹配到其专长领域，随成本敏感性上升将工作转移至更廉价的智能体，并持续优于单模型、集中式编排和市场基线。诊断并修正市场失灵问题（如成本自估计不准确和次优竞标）带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: 通过市场化机制实现去中心化的LLM智能体编排是可行的，且比集中式方法更高效、更鲁棒，为构建更高效的智能体经济指明了方向。

Abstract: As LLM agents proliferate, built by different parties and with different capabilities and costs, orchestrating them is more like assembling labor across the economy than a computer calling a subroutine. Existing orchestration is typically centralized, with a single planner assigning every task, but this creates a bottleneck as agent pools grow, requires private information (e.g., agents' execution costs), and can easily be manipulated, such that a single inserted preference nearly doubles a favored agent's task share under a centralized LLM allocator. We introduce AgentLance, a repeated labor market in which agents bid on tasks using their private costs and self-maintained strategy notes, an allocator selects winners from bids and public reputation records, and a VCG-style payment rule rewards cost-aware bidding. Complex tasks are handled by hierarchical delegation: winning agents can decompose work and subcontract it through the same mechanism. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, knowledge-intensive QA, and agentic tasks, AgentLance matches agents to their specializations, shifts work toward cheaper agents as cost sensitivity rises, and consistently outperforms single-model, centralized-orchestration, and market baselines. Diagnosing market failures, including inaccurate cost self-estimation and sub-optimal bidding, then correcting them in controlled experiments yields further gains, charting a path toward more efficient agent economies.

</details>


### [237] [Agentopia on a Consumer GPU: A Reduced-Scale Long-Horizon Port with an 8B Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24215)
*Luo Huan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文在单张NVIDIA RTX 5070 Ti（12GB显存）上使用4-bit量化模型Qwen3-8B-AWQ实现并评估了缩减规模的Agentopia多智能体社会模拟系统，通过三项结构适配（分层记忆压缩、每日四个活动块、显式身心健康状态变量），三次随机运行均完成约50-52周模拟，无智能体死亡或健康警告，并对比了记忆开关与时间块划分对行为产出的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原版Agentopia在100个智能体、10个模拟年中使用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B进行评估，但其在消费级硬件上的缩减规模部署行为尚不清楚，作者希望探索在普通硬件条件下多智能体社会模拟的可行性。

Method: 在单张RTX 5070 Ti（12GB VRAM）上使用Qwen3-8B-AWQ（4-bit量化模型）运行缩减版Agentopia，引入三层结构适配：系统管理的分层记忆压缩、每模拟日四个活动块、显式物理与心理健康状态变量。进行三次独立随机运行，并设置52周记忆关闭对照实验和10周每日时间块对比实验。

Result: 三次运行中两次完成52周、一次完成50周，总计154个系统周（770个智能体周）。无智能体死亡，无阈值健康警告。含至少一个NO_RESPONSE字段的活动记录占比10.15%-10.29%。记忆关闭实验表明L2/L3工件产出与分层记忆相关；每日四个时间块使最终记录数量提高2.72倍，词汇重复率降低，但缺失字段率更高。

Conclusion: 缩减规模的多智能体模拟在消费级硬件上可行，但对比实验不支持因果行为论断。作者发布了验证配置、审计数据、分析脚本和实现代码（公开分支），原始运行数据和个人初始数据因来源问题未公开。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent social simulation has demonstrated compelling results, but Agentopia was evaluated with 100 agents over 10 simulated years using Qwen3.5-397B-A17B, leaving the behavior of reduced-scale deployments on consumer hardware unclear. In this paper, we implement and evaluate a reduced-scale Agentopia port on a single NVIDIA RTX 5070 Ti(12 GB VRAM) using Qwen3-8B-AWQ, a 4-bit quantized model. We introduce three structural adaptations for this setting: (1) system-managed layered memory compression, (2) four activity blocks per simulated day, and (3) explicit physical- and mental-health state variables. Across three independent stochastic runs, two runs completed 52 weeks and the third completed 50 weeks before reaching the context limit, totaling 154 system-weeks (770 agent-weeks). No agent died,and no threshold-based health warning was logged; activity records containing at least one NO_RESPONSE field occurred at rates of 10.15-10.29% across runs. A 52-week memory-off run tied L2/L3 artifact production to layered memory; a separate 10-week comparison associated four daily time blocks with 2.72 times more finalized records and lower lexical duplication, but a higher missing-field rate. These comparisons do not support causal behavioral claims. We release validated configurations, derived audits, analysis scripts, aggregate figure data, and our implementation changes in a public fork; raw runs and initial persona data are excluded because their redistribution provenance is not fully resolved.

</details>


### [238] [Test-Time Collaborative Classification over Multi-Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24787)
*Ping Hu,Mert Kayaalp,Ali H. Sayed*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种多智能体网络的分布式二分类协作框架，智能体独立训练后在测试阶段通过交换局部决策统计量进行协同推理，并建立了理论上的分类误差保证和PAC风格泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统的异质性不断增加，使得联合训练全局模型面临重大挑战；同时，智能体之间的协作推理被认为是分布式决策的强大机制。作者希望探索一种不需要联合训练、而是通过测试时协作来提升分类性能的框架。

Method: 提出独立训练与协作推理（Independent Training and Cooperative Inference）范式：一组独立训练的智能体（可在架构、特征空间或模态上存在差异）在测试阶段通过分布式学习协议交换局部决策统计量，形成集体预测，并分析不同通信预算和分布式学习规则下的性能。

Result: 建立了在充分通信、有限轮次和有限精度通信条件下的分类误差保证，以及PAC风格泛化界。结果表明模型异质性、网络拓扑、组合策略和通信约束对预测精度的影响，并通过实验揭示了独立训练的代价与集体预测的收益。

Conclusion: 该框架同时展示了独立训练的代价和集体预测的好处，为分布式多智能体决策提供了理论支撑，尤其适用于智能体架构或数据模态存在差异的应用场景。

Abstract: The increasing heterogeneity of multi-agent systems poses significant challenges for jointly training a global model across agents. At the same time, cooperative inference between agents has long been recognized as a powerful mechanism for distributed decision making over networks. Motivated by these observations, we propose a collaboration framework for distributed binary classification over multi-agent networks, where a set of independently trained agents, potentially differing in architecture, feature space, or modality, coordinate their actions during test time to form collective predictions. This coordination is achieved by exchanging local decision statistics through a distributed learning protocol. We develop a theoretical and experimental study of this independent training and cooperative inference paradigm, and examine its performance under different communication budgets and distributed learning rules. We establish classification error guarantees under sufficient, finite-round, and finite-precision communication, together with PAC-style generalization bounds. These results capture the influence of model heterogeneity, network topology, combination policy, and communication constraints on prediction accuracy. Taken together with the experimental results, they reveal both the price of independent training and the benefit of collective prediction for the proposed distributed decision making framework with models learned from data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RENDER基准，通过控制"读者可见的输入形式"来评估记忆和RAG系统，发现输入表示形式（如聊天记录条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始对话）对模型回答准确率有显著影响，最高可达72.6分的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆和RAG评估往往将输入形式视为实现细节，但同一历史内容以不同形式呈现（如记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录）可能显著影响模型性能，缺乏对此的系统性研究。

Method: 构建RENDER基准，包含五级"数据包阶梯"（packet ladder）用于定位答案内容进入输入的位置，并使用确定性模板模拟ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT风格类型化记录和原始对话。在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上进行评估，同时引入检索噪声并在HotpotQA上验证迁移性。

Result: 匹配预算的resolved packets比截断原始对话高42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板中每个模型最佳与最差差距为24.6-48.8分；ChatGPT风格条目在9个模型中的7个上优于原始对话；三个在形式化账本包上得分0%的模型在自然语言条目上达到45.4-53.4%。效果在检索噪声下持续存在并迁移到HotpotQA。

Conclusion: 记忆/RAG评估应报告或控制"读者可见的工件"（reader-facing artifact），因为输入表示形式对模型性能有显著且一致的影响，忽略这一变量可能导致评估结论失真。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [240] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ESQ-Bench，一个面向企业级Oracle数据库的NL2SQL基准测试，包含三个复杂度层级、六个跨数据库填充模式（共465张表）、四个评估指标（EM、EX、SR、SD）以及550个经人工验证的问题-查询对。实验表明GPT-4o在Tier-1/2/3上的执行匹配率分别为79.8/60.3/57.2%，Claude Sonnet 4.6全面超越GPT-4o，而本地开源模型Llama 3.2表现极差（仅13.3%），揭示了闭源API模型与开源权重模型在企业级Oracle场景下的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准（如Spider、BIRD）虽报告超过89%的执行准确率，但其依赖简化的学术模式和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的复杂性。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建更贴近真实企业场景的评估基准。

Method: 构建了Oracle优先的NL2SQL基准ESQ-Bench，包含三个系统化复杂度层级、六个填充模式（Oracle/PostgreSQL/MySQL/SQL Server），采用四指标评估框架（EM精确匹配、EX执行匹配、SR成功率、SD静默分歧），以及550个黄金验证问答对，并在GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Llama 3.2上进行了schema-linked prompting与zero-shot对比实验。

Result: GPT-4o schema-linked在三个层级EX分别为79.8/60.3/57.2%，呈单调下降；EM全程低于7%；静默分歧在EX通过查询中高达73-99%。Claude Sonnet 4.6在每层均超越GPT-4o（87.4/74.9/68.7%）。GPT-4o zero-shot在Tier-2/3上表现更高（73.5/77.8%），但归因于低执行率和幸存者偏差。Llama 3.2仅达13.3%的银行级EX。

Conclusion: 企业级Oracle场景下，现有模型在EM上的极低表现和EX通过查询中高比例的静默分歧，说明NL2SQL模型离真正可靠的企业应用仍有很大距离；闭源API模型与开源模型之间存在显著性能鸿沟，需要针对企业级复杂模式进行专门的基准设计和模型改进。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [241] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体框架，让LLM智能体通过受控实验与科学仿真模型交互，用于制药工艺设计，相比纯语言推理生成更具体、正确的可执行建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与工程任务不仅需要LLM生成合理的文本和代码，还需要理解系统对干预的响应，这依赖于受控实验，而纯语言推理无法满足这一需求。

Method: 提出多智能体框架，将LLM与高保真仿真模型耦合，构建结构化任务表示、设计实验、执行对比仿真、解释结果并综合基于证据的工艺参数优化建议。

Result: 在工业应用场景中，该框架相比纯语言推理具有更高的输出特异性，以及更好的用户评分的正确性和有用性。消融研究和可视化案例分析进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 将仿真集成到实验推理中能够显著增强LLM在科学任务上的实际应用能力，产生比纯语言推理更具体和可操作的输出。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning. In an industrial application setting, this advantage is reflected in higher output specificity as well as improved user-rated correctness and helpfulness. Ablation studies and visualized case analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of simulation-integrated experimental reasoning.

</details>


### [242] [Autonomous Mathematical Discovery in an Open-World Multi-Agent Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23691)
*Stephen Chung,Wenyu Du,William J. Wesley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个名为"Station"的开放世界多智能体环境，其中来自不同模型家族的AI代理在没有中央协调器的情况下共同追求数学研究目标。该系统在12个来自AlphaEvolve目录的构造问题中，在5个问题上取得了优于现有文献的新结果，包括新的有限域Kakeya集无限族、精确604点11维kissing构型、离散Kakeya针和符号不确定性问题的记录，以及Erdős最小重叠问题的改进下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI能否在开放、无预设脚本的环境中自主进行数学发现，替代传统的有固定流水线的自动化证明系统，从而推动数学研究的前沿。

Method: 构建Station多智能体开放世界环境：多个来自不同模型家族的AI代理共享研究目标，自主选择研究方向、设计实验、协作交流并构建共享科学文献，无需中央协调器或脚本化流水线。

Result: 在12个AlphaEvolve构造问题中，5个问题获得了相对现有文献的新结果：新的有限域Kakeya集无限族、11维604点精确kissing构型、离散Kakeya针和符号不确定性问题的记录、Erdős最小重叠问题的改进下界，以及Book Ramsey数的新无限族。代理还生成了定理和分析来解释构造的工作原理。

Conclusion: 开放世界多智能体环境能够产出可解释、可验证的数学发现，不仅提供数值构造，还提供理论解释，证明了去中心化的AI协作可以进行有意义的自主数学研究。

Abstract: We study autonomous mathematical discovery in the Station, an open-world multi-agent environment in which AI agents from different model families pursue a shared research goal without a central coordinator or scripted pipeline. Agents choose their own research directions, conduct experiments, collaborate, and build a shared scientific literature. Across 12 construction problems from the AlphaEvolve catalogue and two additional case studies, the Station obtained results novel relative to the prior literature on five problems: a new infinite family of finite-field Kakeya sets, new exact 604-point kissing configurations in dimension 11, new records for the discretized Kakeya needle and sign uncertainty problems, and a substantially improved lower bound for Erdős's minimum-overlap problem. Agents also discovered novel infinite families for Book Ramsey numbers. Importantly, the agents produced not only numerical constructions but also theorems and analyses explaining how those constructions work, making the results more interpretable and easier for mathematicians to build upon. We release all raw agent dialogues, proofs, and verification code, providing a transparent record of how these discoveries emerged.

</details>


### [243] [A survey detection channel overrides the pixels in an astronomical foundation model, and biases tomographic mean redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23626)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了AION-1（一个训练于2亿多天体的39模态天文transformer）的系统性偏差，发现其严重继承训练数据中目录的不完整性：模型输出主要受检测门控（目标是否在场中心）驱动，而非实际图像内容；目录遗漏导致的红移估计偏差可超LSST DESC基准8.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文基础模型同时使用巡天像素和从像素推导出的目录产品进行训练，而目录本身存在可测量的不完整性比率，模型会继承这种不完整性作为系统性偏差。作者希望量化并审计这一系统性偏差的影响程度。

Method: 对AION-1（39模态transformer，训练于2亿多目标）进行输入因果干预审计：保持图像token逐字节不变，仅编辑巡天分割图；比较检测存在性vs掩膜闭合光的因果驱动；分析322个真实混合天体中流水线光分配的影响；传播3.68%目录遗漏率到层析红移估计；评估解算器（tokeniser）分辨率和稀疏字典的因果可靠性。

Result: 保持图像不变仅编辑分割图可使模型报告的所有量（通量、大小、椭率、红移）改变110-4400倍安慰剂效应的量级；检测门控（r=0.47）比掩膜覆盖光（r=0.30）更具驱动性；矛盾目录光度比无元数据使模型差9倍；3.68%的遗漏率传播使层析红移偏差中位数为LSST DESC要求的0.71倍（40个分配中12个超限），最差bin达8.3倍；光谱学可消除该效应，删除检测通道无代价，效应随模型规模增大；图像codec仅28个有效状态（频谱codec为934），红移读出受量化限制；稀疏字典恢复率仅26-75%，种子变化可移动18个点。

Conclusion: 天文基础模型系统性地将像素级知识外包给分割/检测流水线，其不完整性作为系统性偏差被模型放大继承。这是架构性问题而非微调可修复的问题：目录遗漏率在天文下游科学分析（如层析红移）中产生超出任务要求的偏差。解决方案包括提供光谱信息或移除检测通道（无测量代价），同时tokeniser分辨率和稀疏字典的不可靠性也需要改进。

Abstract: Foundation models for astronomy are trained on survey pixels together with the catalogue products derived from those pixels. Those catalogues are incomplete at a measurable rate, and a model trained on both inherits that incompleteness as a systematic. We audit AION-1, a 39-modality transformer trained on more than 200 million objects, using causal interventions on its inputs.
  Holding the image tokens byte-identical and editing only the survey segmentation map changes every quantity the model reports -- flux, size, ellipticity, redshift -- by 110-4400 times a matched placebo. The mechanism is detection gating, presence at the field centre (r = 0.47), not the light the mask encloses (r = 0.30); across 322 real blends the model ignores how the pipeline partitioned the light (R = -0.006). Nor is the preference specific to that channel: contradicted catalogue photometry leaves the model nine times worse than supplying no metadata at all.
  The Legacy Survey pipeline leaves 3.68% of targets with no segment covering their position. Propagating that rate, with a miss represented by the fields the pipeline actually returns, shifts tomographic mean redshifts by a median 0.71 times the LSST DESC requirement over 40 assignments and exceeds it in 12; observed positional errors take the worst bin to 8.3 times. Drawing the misses by their measured magnitude dependence rather than uniformly does not change it. Spectroscopy removes the effect, withholding the detection channel removes it at no measurable cost, and the effect grows with model scale.
  Two further limits lie in the tokeniser: its image codec resolves 28 effective states on source patches against 934 for the spectrum codec, and the redshift readout is quantisation-limited. Sparse dictionaries are unreliable causal handles: across 15, recovery spans 26-75% and moves up to 18 points on the seed alone.

</details>


### [244] [MARS: Multi-Specialist LLM Relay System for Competitive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23918)
*Andrei Mikhailov,Mikhail Burtsev,Alsu Sagirova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARS（多代理专业LLM接力框架），每个代理充当算法主题专家（如动态规划、图论、字符串等），通过检索增强生成从算法理论语料库中挑选相关专家团队，在沙箱中迭代运行候选代码并交接给下一个专家，最终在CodeContests测试集上达到0.624的通过率，比直接提示提升14.4个百分点，并以3.3倍更低的耗时接近CodeSIM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型代码生成在多代理流水线中仅使用通用的规划者、编码者和调试者角色，算法技术的选择完全依赖骨干模型本身，导致在竞争性编程中持续存在失效模式。需要一种将算法专业知识显式注入多代理框架的方法。

Method: MARS框架中的每个代理都是特定算法主题的专家（动态规划、图、字符串、几何等），通过检索增强生成（RAG）基于算法理论语料库进行检索。给定问题后，检索机制选择相关专家小团队，起始代理编写初始C++17解决方案，随后每个轮次在沙箱中针对公开样例运行候选代码，由当前活跃专家决定保留、修复或移交草稿，并将结构化信息包传递给下一个专家，最后由基础设施修复代理规范化样板代码。

Result: 在CodeContests测试集上使用Gemma 4，MARS达到0.624±0.006的通过率，每任务平均2.3个流水线阶段，比直接提示提升14.4个百分点；接近CodeSIM（0.731）的性能，同时时间成本低3.3倍，且每任务token消耗的方差显著更小。

Conclusion: MARS表明通过主题专业化代理和算法知识检索增强，可以显著提升LLM在竞争性编程中的代码生成能力，以更低的计算成本获得接近最先进方法的性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models excel at code generation, yet competitive programming exposes a persistent failure mode: existing multi-agent pipelines distribute work over generic planner, coder, and debugger roles and delegate the choice of algorithmic technique to the backbone alone. We present MARS (Multi-Agent Relay of Specialized LLMs), a prompt-only framework in which each agent is a topic specialist---dynamic programming, graphs, strings, geometry, and so on---grounded by retrieval-augmented generation over an algorithm-theory corpus. Given a problem, retrieval selects a small team of relevant specialists; a starter writes an initial C++17 solution, and each subsequent turn runs the candidate against public examples in a sandbox, lets the active specialist keep, repair, or hand off the draft, and forwards a structured packet to the next specialist. A single infrastructure-fixer pass normalizes boilerplate at the end. On the CodeContests test split with Gemma 4, MARS reaches $0.624 \pm 0.006$ pass rate at $2.3$ recorded pipeline stages per task ($+14.4$ percentage points over direct prompting), closing most of the gap to CodeSIM ($0.731$) at $3.3{\times}$ lower wall-clock cost and substantially smaller variance in per-task token spend. The source code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/fckand/mars.

</details>


### [245] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*
